(3) FL repair delayed a yr, not dead — Wells FY28 bull FL 7–8% OM (+200bp Nike allocation, +150bp merchandising, +100bp occupancy, +100bp personnel); JPM FY27 FL OP $103M ≈ low end of prior FY26 guide; UBS 5-scenario FL framework from "missed opportunity" (1–2% OM) to "true bull" (>4% OM). (4) Valuation at trough — Barclays "Street NTM P/E" 8.3x at low; JPM 5–6x EV/EBITDA "trough valuation range"; UBS: stock embedded ~$13.50 FY27 EPS at 15x pre-print. (5) Bears (BMO $110 UP; Truist $128 Hold; Baird, Telsey downgrades): structural footwear headwinds, FL cash drag, margin risk; UBS buy-side FY27 EPS "bulls ~$13.00", "bears ~$10.00."
- Under-weighted (author read): Street never modelled new debt (all held debt flat; Wells bull had $1B paydown); Sept $1B adds ~$66M/yr coupon, presumably for buybacks not modelled — JPM $497M FY26 buybacks post-print; BBG ~$252M. Core margin erosion attributed to "promotions"; SG&A/D&A/relocation compression untested. Comp quality: only Wells digs into 3 definition changes & +200–275bp relocation; UBS ~150bp HoS only other. Tariff refunds flattered Q2 core GM ~50bp, FY27 compare.

### 8.1 Expert calls (14 AlphaSense transcripts, 2026-06-13 to ~07-29)
- Most credible: #3 24-yr Nike planner (AlphaSense metadata "Retail Shoe Chain"); #8 Under Armour Director of Merchandising, Americas; #10 Jordan Brand Geo Merchandising Director supplying DKS, FL, Champs. These most muted: Nike sell-in 0–5%, UA MSD-HSD, Jordan "conservative." Eye-catching growth (+238%, +150%, +14–16%) from base effects, new accounts, low-reliability sources.
| Expert claim (pre-print) | Outcome 2026-08-25 | Verdict |
|---|---|---|
| UA #8 (2026-07-10): "DICK'S and the marketplace at large are definitely getting more promotional ... That's more of a gross margin situation on their side." | DICK'S margin guide cut 40bp on promos | Right; only pre-print warning; AlphaSense summary omitted it |
| DKS asking vendors for "back-end protection" (#8); ~40% at-once buys (#4); only August committed (#7); auto-replenishment (#4, #8) | DKS inv +6% vs sales +5.6%; open-to-buy flexible | Right on DKS caution |
| Stable trade terms; DKS requires inbound margin (#12) but self-funds markdowns on MAP brands w/o margin agreements (UGG #13) | margin hit on DKS P&L | Consistent |
| FL-exposed vendors positive on FL (Jordan #10, others) | FL comp −3.6%; FL FY guide to loss | Wrong on direction |
| Running/performance & brown shoe (UGG, Birkenstock) strong; lifestyle retro weak | Stack "hangover" lifestyle; strength performance | Right |
| World Cup closeouts; UA fleece soft at $120–150 | Q2 comp incl "strong results from the 2026 FIFA World Cup" | Partly |
- Q3 implications (derived): (i) established brands' Aug–Oct sell-in planned LSD–MSD + heavy UGG/fall receipts vs gdnc 2H DICK'S sales −0.2% to +2.5% → unless OTB cut post-print, inv likely growing ≥ sales at Q3 end; (ii) Sep–Oct seasonal low post World Cup/BTS (UA "bigger moments drive more volume"); (iii) markdown exposure on DKS P&L; (iv) performance running & brown shoe offsets; (v) no vendor data on FL Q3; FL guide untested.
- Caveats: interviews ended by 2026-07-29 (before late-qtr promo escalation); Aug–Oct numbers are futures orders placed 6–9 mo earlier; AlphaSense metadata wrong in places; existing quote bank (`DKS_Expert_Calls_AlphaSense_Key_Insights_and_Quote_Bank.md`) mislabels Nike planner (is 07-06 interview, not 07-14).

### 8.2 Alt data (Bloomberg ALTD pulled 2026-10-04)
- BSM card spend tracked DKS reported rev within 1.56pt mean abs miss outside acquisition qtrs; missed badly in two FL-close qtrs (−16.4pt, −13.15pt).
- FL lap trap: weekly BSM +15% to +58% y/y every week w/e 2025-10-05 to 2026-09-06 (FL entered panel); post 2026-09-08 anniversary −3.3 / −2.4 / −3.3 (w/e 9/13, 9/20, 9/27) vs Sporting Goods Stores −8.3, Retail-Discretionary −3.3 same 3 wks. W/e 9/27: spend −3.33, count −4.45, customers −5.86, ticket +1.17. Ticket decel +8.0 (Oct-25) → +3.49 (Aug-26), matching cons ticket path 5.50 → 3.60 → 1.25E → 1.00E.
- Translation (derived): pre-deal raw BSM 3–8pt below reported growth; post-deal ~31pt below (FL inorganic not captured); post-lap applying pre-deal gap to −3.0% → reported growth ~0% to +5% → in line/modestly above Q3 rev cons (≈−0.3% vs PF). BBG 45.97% "adjusted" QTD estimate = pre-lap carry-forward, must not be read as beat.
- August industry: Sporting Goods Stores −11.7% (BSM), −16.5% (Facteus), −6.0% (Placer foot traffic); DKS beat category by +34pt (BSM), +7pt (Placer). Pre-lap Q3 weeks DKS avg +23.0 (BSM weekly).
- Caveats: Facteus "organic" DKS-only proxy inference not supported (ran above reported comps post-deal: Q4 FY25 +7.2 vs 3.1). Placer post-lap −4.4 / +4.8 / −3.8. Alt data can't give DICK'S vs FL split, margins/promos, FL EMEA (US-only panels).

### 8.3 Reconciling with Aug-25 print; Nov implications
- Aug-25 = margin event not sales: DICK'S comp +4.9% vs cons 4.5–4.9%; rev $5,586.8M vs cons ~$5,646–5,650M (FL comp the miss); core OM 12.6% vs ~12.3–12.4% cons (flattered by refunds); FL OM −1.8% vs −0.4% to −0.5%; EPS $3.53 vs $3.75–3.77. Primary research flagged margin (UA), defensive buying, FL blind spot; alt data couldn't.
- Nov-24/25 (derived): (1) revenue unlikely problem — post-lap card ~0–5% vs cons ≈−0.3% PF; small upside skew to DICK'S comp vs 1.69%; (2) GM swing factor — JPM 90bp promo pressure; vendors not funding markdowns; cons DICK'S OI margin −175bp y/y (7.17% vs 8.92%) adequately bearish vs JPM 2017 analog −200bp; (3) Q3-end inventory = tell; cons inv $5,860M (+3.9% y/y); ≥ that with ~flat PF sales → promotional Q4; (4) FL unknown; cons FL OI −$71.3M.

### 9. Debate map
- Sensitivities (derived; ~87M avg FY27 sh, 28% tax):
| Lever FY27 | EPS | Value at 12x |
|---|---|---|
| ±$50M FL OI | ±$0.41 | ±$5/sh |
| ±50bp DICK'S seg margin (on ~$15.2B) | ±$0.63 | ±$7.5/sh |
| ±1pt DICK'S comp at ~25% incremental flow-through | ±$0.31 | ±$3.7/sh |
| ±1pt tax (on ~$1.55B EBT) | ±$0.18 | ±$2/sh |
| $1B buyback at $140 (7.1M sh), net of 4% forgone interest | ≈+$0.75 (from $12.60 base) | — |
| ±1x P/E on ~$12.7 | — | ±$12.7/sh |
- Debate 1 FL endgame (±$15–25/sh): at $137.34, 12x core-only EPS (~$12.6) implies FL ≈ −$1.1B; at 7x core EBITDA ≈ −$1.2B; at 6.5x ≈ −$0.1B (derived).
  - Bull (Wells, UBS scen 3–4, mgmt "menu"): collapse mostly Nike allocation (~490bp of 880bp); Nike back to wholesale (NA wholesale +14% FY26) needs FL; Fast Break stores comp "+DD with improving merch margin" (Wells); FL margins 3–4% by FY28, 7–8% Wells bull; severable losers: EMEA 559 stores, comps −10% 2025, ~4pts tax drag; WSS 151 → 99; Champs 364 stores, >75% within a mile of FL; FL capex ~$0.4B; short leases; Stack "simply yes," "creating a menu … no preconceived ideas" (2026-09-14).
  - Bear (BMO $110, Barclays "TRAP", Wells "is proving to be an albatross"): structurally declining mall chain; biggest supplier building DTC & broadening distribution (Famous Footwear Jordan +63%, possible Decathlon); Europe exit slow/expensive; FL absorbs mgmt time, capital (~$0.4B), inventory ($2.0B from $1.7B), tax rate; losses worsening (Q2 FY26 −$31.9M).
  - Evidence: FL PF comps −4.7/−3.4/+0.6/−3.6%; FY26 guide +$100–150M → −$80 to −$40M; charges $515.8M, "up to $750M"; stores 2,347 → 2,212; FL Intl President bought $499,869 on 9/30.
  - Tests: Q3 call (Nov 24) store-closure count/EMEA review/capex cut; FL Q4 FY26 (first qtr in comp base) positive vs −3.4% Q4 FY25 clearance compare; Q4 goodwill/tradename impairment (~$591M + $710M); Nike Investor Day (Nov 16–17) re wholesale doors.
  - Sensitivity: FL −1% vs +1.6% OM on ~$7.35B ≈ $190M EBIT ≈ ±$1.55 EPS; FL at 0 vs −$1.1B ≈ +$12/sh; at +$1B (≈0.15x sales or ~4x normalized $250M EBITDA) ≈ +$23/sh (derived).
  - Brokers: Wells & JPM FL LT bulls (JPM FY27 FL OP $103M); Barclays & UBS near-term sceptical; BMO bearish; no broker modelled EMEA exit/licensing.
- Debate 2 core DICK'S margin, cyclical vs structural (±$7–12/sh):
  - Bull (Street): FY25 seg margin 11.12% flat vs FY24 11.14%; FY26 cut (−$70M) promo-driven/temporary; JPM 2017 analog −200bp then −100bp then recovery; mix tailwinds (vertical +700–900bp, GameChanger ~80% contribution margin, DMN profitable); HoS cohorts mature/lever; FY27 cons DICK'S OI +5.9% (margin ≈10.83% derived).
  - Bear (author variant): seg margin fell 3 of 4 post-close qtrs at comps 3.1–6.0%; SG&A levered 1 of 7; capex ~2.3x D&A, HoS D&A wave ahead; Q2 +79bp GM incl ~50bp tariff refunds, ex-refund Q2 seg margin ≈−96bp; ticket 70–100% of comp, Q4 FY25 transactions −1.3%; underlying comp ex relocations & GameChanger +1.0–1.5% (Wells) → box not levering; growth bought with capex.
  - Tests: Q3 SG&A $ growth vs sales (cons DICK'S OI margin 7.17%, ≈−175bp); Q4 SG&A leverage at ~1.6% comp; FY27 core margin guide March 2027 (expansion ex-refunds?); HoS yr 2–4 cohort productivity (Lasser asked; mgmt declined).
  - Sensitivity: FY27 core margin 10.0% vs cons ~10.8% ≈ −$1.00 EPS; 11.1% ≈ +$0.40 (derived). All 4 main brokers treat core as safe half; Wells comp-quality work exception but stays OW.
- Debate 3 footwear/Nike sell-in correction duration (timing; drives Q3/Q4, FY27 shape):
  - Bull: Nike ~400bp 2Q FY27 pressure lapping "higher North America sell-in"; correction peaks Sep–Nov 2026 (BBG cons NA wholesale −10.4%); Spring 2027 order books "growth-oriented for NKE, Asics, Brooks, and Hoka" (DB US Contact #2); channel cleaner 1H27 → JPM "own the clean up."
  - Bear: >30% Nike assortment discounted; Sportswear/Jordan reset "beyond fiscal 2027"; lifestyle franchises (AF1, Dunk, AJ1) no replacement; Williams Trading: prices >$150 hard to commercialize; FL "more dependent on launch and retro product."
  - Evidence: Nike NA wholesale +11/+24/+11/+9.8%/+9%. Tests: Nike FQ2 FY27 (~mid/late Dec 2026), Investor Day (Nov 16–17); DKS Q3 inventory; JD NA LFL (JPM −6% Q3 / −5% Q4).
  - Sensitivity: ±1pt 2H DICK'S comp ≈ ±$0.15 FY26 EPS; FL GM ±100bp on ~$3.88B 2H FL sales ≈ ±$0.31 (derived). JPM & Barclays expect clarity "during 2027"; Wells "much more challenged athletic footwear backdrop into 2H."
- Debate 4 capital allocation / $1B / dividend (±$0.75 EPS; sentiment):
  - Bull: "repurchases of shares" in use of proceeds; $3.0B authorization; 7 insiders $5.59M incl CFO ~$1M on pricing day; ASR at ~10x FY27 EPS +~6% accretive (derived).
  - Bear: LTM FCF $239.4M vs ~$450M dividend; FY26 net capex ~$1.4B; FL WC absorbing cash; $1B = liquidity insurance; buying back while borrowing at 6.2–6.9% with FL burning cash = rating risk ("Rating risk could emerge," BI 2026-09-30); dividend only +3% 2026.
  - Tests: buyback/ASR/10b5-1 at Q3; Q3 cash ($647.95M cons pre-notes, ~$1.6B with); rating-agency commentary; FY27 capex guide (≤$1.2B net = change).
  - Sensitivity: $1B at $140 ≈ +$0.75 FY27 EPS; holding cash ≈ +$0.30 interest income vs ~$0.53 new coupon after tax (derived). No equity model incl new debt; JPM $497M FY26 buybacks; BBG ~$252M.
- Debate 5 comp quality & HoS returns (multiple 10x vs 14x):
  - Bull: HoS ~$35M yr-1 sales, ~$7M EBITDA, ~25% CoC, <4-yr payback (mgmt); Wells HoS ROIC 27.2%, Field House 42.1%; fleet 41 HoS / 52 FH Aug-2026 → 49/63 (FY26E) → 68/81 (FY27E) (BBG).
  - Bear: gross of cannibalization; ~75% of openings relocations in comps; comp definition changed 3x, cumulative 110bp+ lift (Wells); 75–100 target silently dropped (~67); Wells: $25M sales → ROIC 9.9%.
  - Tests: cohort disclosure; Placer visits by store (old box vs relocated HoS); comp holding +2–3% when relocations slow. Each 1x ≈ $12.7/sh. UBS HoS ~150bp; Wells +200–275bp relocations; others accept reported comp.
- Debate 6 credibility & governance (multiple discount):
  - Bull: core guide beaten 3 yrs (FY24, FY25 raised & beaten); insiders buying; Stack 2017–21 record.
  - Bear: FL gdnc raised then slashed within 3 mo; accretion claim gone; HoS targets dropped; $49.0M aircraft from Stack LLC; withhold vs Stack 1.67M → 10.77M; Class B ~78% votes, authorized Class B raised 5x; Hobart sold $4.58M at ~$228 in May; CEO/Chair didn't buy post-crash.
  - Test: Q3/Q4 delivery vs lowered guide; FL action scope. Sensitivity: 1–2x multiple discount vs history (~13x avg per Wells) ≈ $13–25/sh.

### 10. Author's take
- LONG at $137.34; 12–24-mo base $154 (+12%) + $5.00/yr dividend (3.6%) ≈ +15% total; bull $209 (+52%); bear $93 (−32%); prob-weighted (50/25/25) $152 (+11% price; ~+14–15% w/ dividends); skew ≈1.6:1. Start half position pre-Nov-24 print; add on FL action or buyback; won't size up ahead of March-2027 FY27 guide (expected below cons).
- Why long though cons too high: at ~10–11x author FY27 EPS stock discounts de-rated core AND FL ≈ −$1.1B; core somewhat worse than Street (V1) but FL less bad than price (V2); second effect larger w/ nearer catalyst.
- Nov-24 print probabilities:
| Outcome | Prob | Basis |
|---|---|---|
| Q3 adj EPS ≥ cons $1.39 | 40% | base: DICK'S sales ~$3,330M (comp ~2–2.5%), DICK'S margin ~7.2%, FL margin ~−4.2% on ~$1,735M, net interest −$15M, tax 29%, 89.9M sh → ~$1.20 (range $0.87 bad / $1.54 good) |
| DICK'S comp ≥1.69% | 65% | post-lap card 0–5% reported total growth; DKS beat sub-industry ~5pt post-lap |
| FY26 EPS guide raised | 5% | — |
| Maintained $11.00–12.00 | 40% | — |
| Narrowed, midpoint ≤$11.50 | 35% | — |
| Cut below $11.00 | 20% | Q3 "more difficult than Q4"; Q4 needs $3.32–4.32 if Q3 $1.25 (derived) |
| Concrete FL action at Q3 (closure count, EMEA review, capex cut) | 40%; 65% by March 2027 | "simply yes"/"menu"; 67 closures so far; new Intl president |
| Buyback ≥$500M or ASR by March 2027 | 55% | use of proceeds; insider cluster; no Q2 buyback |
| Stock up on print day | 50%; expected move ±9.5–10% | EPS cut 53% in 60 days; SI 13.7%; flat skew; 10,790 Nov-115 puts (expire pre-print) |
- March-2027 FY27 guide (Q4 FY26 print ~Mar 10–12 2027): 65% midpoint below then-current cons (now $13.36); author expects ~$12.00–13.50 (buyback helps; IEEPA lap, World Cup lap, new coupon, FL "investment" hurt). March guides historically conservative: Mar-2026 $13.50–14.50 vs Barclays-cited cons $14.76, stock +1.06% that day. Main near-term downside event; credible FL simplification plan would neutralize.
- FY27 scenarios (dks_scenario_model.py):
| | Core sales/margin | FL sales/margin | Net interest | Tax | Avg sh | FY27 EPS | Multiple | Value |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Bull 25% | $15.35B / 11.1% | $7.45B / +1.6% | −$111M | 27% | 83.0M (~$1B buyback) | $14.93 | 14x (pre-FL avg ~13–15x) | ~$209 |
| Base 50% | $15.2B / 10.55% | $7.35B / +0.3% | −$106M | 28% | 85.5M (~$0.7B buyback) | $12.67 (no buyback $12.37) | 12x | ~$152–154 |
| Bear 25% | $15.0B / 9.8% | $7.25B / −0.8% | −$91M | 29% | 89.0M | $10.42 | 9x (ASO 7.4x; JD 6.9x) | ~$93 |
| Consensus | $15.2B / ~10.8% | $7.37B / +0.3% | −$75M (interest exp only) | 27.5% | 89.1M | $13.36 | — | — |
- Cross-checks: base FY27 ~5% below cons mostly core margin (−28bp) and interest (new coupon not loaded); SOTP core-only NI ≈$1.12B ($12.6/sh) at 12x ≈ $151/sh w/ FL at zero (base gives FL no value); FL at +$0.5B adds ~$6/sh.
- V1 detail: Street models FY27 DICK'S margin recovering ~10.8%; author: structurally de-levering at ~10.5% or below — (a) ~half reported comp bought w/ relocation capex, underlying +1.0–1.5%, ticket dominates; (b) SG&A levered 1 of 7 even at 4–6% comps; (c) D&A outgrowing sales: ~$1.0B core net capex vs ~$490M total D&A; (d) FY26 flattered by tariff refunds, World Cup, 1H settlement income (excluded from non-GAAP but cash cushion). → FY27 core OI ~$1.60B not $1.65B; EPS ~$12.4–12.7 pre-buyback. Work: store-level comp decomposition (HoS/FH relocations from 8-Ks, Placer by location); SG&A bridge from Note 17 (personnel 14.0%, other 16.1%, occupancy 8.5%) + new-format per-store opex; D&A schedule from capex vintages; 3–4 expert calls ex-DKS FP&A/store ops re HoS yr-2+ productivity & labor; size IEEPA/World Cup/Knicks lap w/ IR. Disconfirm: SG&A leverage Q3/Q4 at ≤2% comp; FY27 guide core margin expansion ≥30bp ex-refunds; HoS yrs 2–4 comping +5%+.
- V2 detail: price implies FL ≈ −$1.1B; rational only if indefinite cash burn. Severable: International 681 stores (Europe 559, APAC 92, atmos 30), comps −8.1% FY25, valuation-allowed losses inflate group tax ~4pts (≈$58M, $0.65/sh); FL capex ~$0.4B/yr; Champs 364 stores overlapping. Signals: "simply yes," "creating a menu"; Barnes (Aldi Süd, Tesco UK) runs Intl, bought $0.5M on 9/30; $1B liquidity at low; FL leases 5–10 yrs. Credible simplification: indicative $0.9–1.3 EPS (EMEA loss elimination + tax normalization) + ~$2/sh/yr FCF from FL capex to maintenance; EMEA standalone loss not disclosed (key gap); EPS est assumption until geographic P&L rebuilt. FL −$1.1B → ~0 ≈ +$12/sh; proof of 3%+ NA margin path (Fast Break) worth more. Work: rebuild FL P&L by geography from FL FY2021–FY2024 10-Ks (division profit NA/EMEA/APAC) + DKS Note 17, estimate EMEA FY26 loss; map FL Europe lease expiries & closure costs (works councils France, Germany), precedent exit/licensing (FL licensed stores ME/Asia; JD, Snipes, Courir); value atmos & WSS standalone; expert calls ex-FL Europe & ex-Champs; model $288.5M valuation allowance under exit; ask IR whether "menu" incl geographic exits. Disconfirm: Q3/Q4 reaffirmation of global footprint or more FL capex (e.g., Fast Break in EMEA); impairment w/o restructuring plan; Nike Investor Day broad distribution expansion diluting FL NA allocation; Barnes leaving.
- Dated catalysts: ~late Oct 2026 Deckers FQ2 FY27 (HOKA wholesale re-accel; IEEPA refund sharing); ~mid-Nov 2026 On Q3 2026 (US wholesale; key accounts DKS/FL/JD); Nov 16–17 Nike Investor Day (wholesale door targets; Decathlon/family-footwear expansion; FL/DKS partnership); Nov 20 option expiry (pre-print); Nov 24, 2026 (company tentative; vendors 11/25) DKS Q3 FY26 (DICK'S margin vs 7.17% cons; SG&A; inventory vs $5.86B cons; FL actions; buyback/ASR; FY guide); ~Dec 8 Academy Q3 (traffic, promos, Jordan/HOKA door read-across); ~Dec 11 DKS ex-div ($1.25); ~mid/late Dec Nike FQ2 FY27 (Sep–Nov; NA wholesale vs −10.4% cons; inventory; Spring-27 order book); Nov 2026–Jan 2027 Q4 FY26 FL enters comp base (laps −3.4% clearance qtr); ~Mar 10–12, 2027 DKS Q4 FY26 + FY27 guide (EPS vs ~$13.36; core margin; FL plan; capex; impairment; dividend raise); ~Mar 25–27, 2027 10-K (FL segment detail, Nike %, lease ladder, HoS target language); ~June 2027 AGM (withhold trend on Stack).

### 11. Gaps (ranked by value of info)
1. FL P&L by geography/banner (EMEA, NA, APAC, WSS, atmos, Champs) — not disclosed post-close; rebuild from FL 10-Ks + DKS Note 17; ask IR. 2. Underlying core comp & HoS cohort economics — no relocation/yr 2–4 disclosure; Placer store-level + ex-DKS real estate/FP&A call. 3. Real-time Q3 promo/markdown depth DICK'S & FL — JPM tracks daily; build tracker (DKS.com, FL.com, ScoreCard+ offers) pre-Nov 24. 4. Use of $1B — ask IR; check 10-Q for repurchases after 9/25. 5. Nike 2027 allocation/distribution — Investor Day; channel checks w/ Nike/Jordan account managers re FL NA vs new doors (Famous Footwear, Decathlon). 6. FL Europe exit costs/options (leases, labor law, licensing precedents). 7. Tax — FL valuation allowance $288.5M at acquisition, $285.8M in 10-K; ETR path. 8. FL inventory quality — $2.0B at 8/1 vs $1.7B at 5/2; aged lifestyle share? 9. Credit — rating stance post $1B; S&P outlook "stable" in FWP vs "positive" per BI (D42). 10. Governance/succession — proxy Class B holdings; Stack succession; aircraft details.
- Library data gaps: DB/Planalytics Q3 weather files no data; BMO UP note (PT $110) not in folder (only rating change); BBG ALTD QTD days 37–56 not captured; Placer same-store visits & Facteus customer data entitlement-locked; no FL-specific vendor Q3 data; AlphaSense metadata errors; Nike-planner quote mislabelled in quote bank.
- Number conflicts: print date Nov 24 (co) vs Nov 25 (vendors); price $137.34 close vs $137.26 intraday; options-implied move ±9.5–10% not 13%; tariff refunds $59.0M received, $38.1M excluded, ~$20.9M retained; FL election 92.6% prelim vs 85.8% final; Nike share 31% (10-K) vs ~37% PF (Wells); BBG FY25 transactions wrong; Yahoo Q3 FY25 EPS base $2.78 (DICK'S-only) vs like-for-like $2.07; "2026 debt" = Sept 2026 issue, no 2026 maturity.

## SRC 00_DKS_SYNTHESIS/DKS_PRIMER_READABLE.md | Oct 2026 (price $137.34) | plain-English rewrite of DKS_PRIMER.md (same author; Point72 Academy pitch) | same coverage as SRC 02
- Content =same as SRC 00_DKS_SYNTHESIS/DKS_PRIMER.md throughout (same call: LONG half position; scenarios bull 25% $14.93 14x ~$209 / base 50% $12.67 12x ~$154 / bear 25% $10.42 9x ~$93; prob-weighted ~$152, +11% price, +14–15% incl 3.6% div yield, 1.6:1 skew; same variant views, debates, sensitivities ±$0.41/±$0.63/±$0.31/+$0.75, catalysts, gaps, traps). Only differences/additions recorded:
- Framing: DKS "the best-run big-box sporting goods retailer in America," one of best retail stocks for ~4 yrs pre-FL deal. "That's not a screaming trade. It's a decent one with a specific reason to exist."
- Share: DKS ~9% of own market definition and ~20% of B&M sporting goods & athletic footwear channel. DKS +~4.5pts share over decade, FL −~3.7pts → combined ≈2016 level: "Dick's didn't buy a growth business. It bought the share donor."
- Core FY25 cost structure restated: merch cost ~half of sales, labor ~14%, rent/occupancy ~8.5%, other ~16%; 2019 OM 5% → margin more than doubled in 6 yrs, ~half merch margin, ~half rent leverage.
- Nike: one NA wholesale qtr +24%; FL share of Nike "high heat" allocation roughly halved; Nike guiding rev down HSD this yr.
- JD Sports NA sales "decline mid-single digits" (primer: ~−7%). Academy traffic fell >5% last qtr while DKS comped nearly 5%.
- Tariff refunds framed as after "the Supreme Court ruling on emergency tariffs"; ~$21M retained flattered core GM ~0.5pt. Gas prices up "on Middle East conflict"; rate expectations flipped cuts → possible hikes. Event laps: 2026 World Cup and "the Knicks' playoff run."
- Dividend +105% 2023, +10% in 2024 and 2025, +3% 2026 to $1.25/qtr. Buybacks $649M FY23, $268M FY24, $342M FY25, $141M Q1 FY26 at ~$196–219; nothing in Q2.
- 2026 notes roughly double annual interest ~$70M → ~$137M; author leans to buyback war-chest reading; "Dick's bonds trade wide of peers."
- Deal: "Dick's did its homework, concluded the business was in worse shape than management claimed, walked away, came back and bought it anyway at 40% less" ($40 → $24); ~86% of FL holders chose stock; total ~$2.5B.
- FL OM 9%–12% FY2018–FY2021, ~8% 2022, ~2.5% 2023 & 2024, then negative; Wells: >half of decline = Nike allocation cut. Lace Up promised 8.5–9% by 2026.
- Post-close FL charges $516M incl $218M inventory write-down in first owned qtr; Fast Break "now in more than 250 stores," 300–350 by YE; Ann Freeman "a longtime Nike North America executive."
- SOTP restated: core ~$12.60 FY27 EPS × 12 ≈ $151/sh ≈ $13.5B vs whole co ~$12.4B → FL ≈ −$1.1B. FL capex ~$400M/yr "could be cut in half." "Even with a below-consensus core and Foot Locker valued at zero, you get about $150. You're getting the Foot Locker option for free, and maybe being paid to take it." Base assumes core margin ~10.5%, FL ~breakeven, full yr new interest, ~$700M buybacks.
- Aug cut: midpoint $14.00 → $11.50; tax 27% → 29% ≈ 35 cents; core margin gdnc 11.0–11.4% → 10.6–10.9%, comp held 2.5–4.0% ("the core's problem is margin, not sales"). 2H implied: core profit ~flat to −5%, FL loss $26–66M, EPS $4.57–5.57; cons $5.01; Q3 cons EPS $1.39 "down a third from last year." FY27 cons: core profit +~6%, FL from $73M loss to $20M profit.
- Summary table (FY22/23/24/25/26E/27E): rev $B 12.4/13.0/13.4/17.2/22.1/22.6; core comp −0.5%/+2.6%/+5.2%/+4.5%/~3.4%/~2.6%; adj EPS $12.04/$12.91/$14.05/$13.20/$11.49/$13.36; FCF $M 558/940/509/400/~500/~458 (FY26–27 BBG cons). Post-close qtr table =same as SRC 02 §6.2 (core margin 8.9%/11.0%/10.7%/12.6%). FL store base ~9% smaller.
- Expert-call vendor set: Nike, Under Armour, Jordan, Crocs, UGG, Carhartt and others; flashy triple-digit numbers from new accounts or "obvious axes to grind." Vendors described DKS asking for markdown protection, buying more last-minute, committing only a month out. Card data: post-anniversary spend ~−3% each of 3 wks vs category ~−8%, discretionary ~−3%; August category −6% to −17% depending on dataset.
- Near-term: Q3 EPS ~$1.20 vs $1.39; 40% beat; 65% core comp beats 1.7%; guidance held/narrowed toward low end 75%; 20% cut below $11; ~40% concrete FL action; stock direction "a coin flip"; 65% FY27 guide below cons; ~55% ≥$500M buyback by March.
- Dates: Early December Academy; early-to-mid March 2027 DKS Q4 + FY27 guide ("the big one"); June 2027 AGM.
- Traps: Nov 24 vs Nov 25; options move ~10% not 13%; "2026 debt" = Sept bond deal; last yr Q3 EPS comp $2.07 not Yahoo $2.78.

### 3. Guidance by release (as printed; "prior" = preceding release). No GM/EBT-margin gdnc except Nov-2023 recon (EBT margin) and FY26 segment-profit margins.
- 2022-11-22 (Q3 FY22) FY2022: comp (3.0)% to (1.5)%; GAAP EPS $10.50–11.10 (not dependent on buybacks beyond $361M through Q3); NG $11.50–12.10 (eliminates if-converted settlement); ~99M GAAP / ~88M NG sh; tax not given (26% for NG adj); capex $400–425M / $340–365M. RAISED: comp from (6.0)% to (2.0)%; GAAP EPS from $8.85–10.55; NG from $10.00–12.00. Recon: GAAP NI $1,015–1,070M + $26M after-tax conv interest = $1,041–1,096M on 99M; NG NI $1,015–1,070M on 88M.
- 2023-03-07 (Q4 FY22) FY2023 (53 wk) initial: comp flat to +2.0% (52-wk); EPS $12.90–13.80 (incl ~$0.20 53rd wk), NG same; ~88M; tax ~22%; capex $670–720M / $550–600M.
- 2023-05-23 (Q1 FY23): maintained; incl Moosejaw; ~88M; tax ~22% → ~21%.
- 2023-08-22 (Q2 FY23): comp flat–+2.0% maintained; recon GAAP EBT $1,239–1,327M, NG EBT $1,259–1,347M; GAAP EPS $11.33–12.13 (incl ~$0.20 53rd wk); NG $11.50–12.30 (excl ~$20M severance from business optimization); possible additional one-time charges $25–50M not included; ~87M; ~21%; capex unch. CUT GAAP EPS from $12.90–13.80; sh from ~88M. Recon NI $986–1,055M GAAP → $1,001–1,070M NG.
- 2023-11-21 (Q3 FY23): comp +0.5% to +2.0%; recon GAAP EBT $1,232–1,297M = 9.7–10.0% of sales; NG EBT $1,295–1,360M = 10.2–10.6%; GAAP EPS $11.45–12.05; NG $12.00–12.60 (excl ~$62.5M business optimization; ~$10M more Q4); ~86M; ~21%. RAISED comp, NG EPS from $11.50–12.30; GAAP low end up/high end down; NI GAAP $980–1,032M → NG $1,026–1,078M.
- 2024-03-14 (Q4 FY23) FY2024 initial: comp +1.0–2.0%; sales $13.0–13.13B; EPS $12.85–13.25 (GAAP=NG); ~83M; ~24%; capex ~$900M / ~$800M.
- 2024-05-29: comp +2.0–3.0%; sales $13.1–13.2B; EPS $13.35–13.75; ~23%. RAISED.
- 2024-09-04: comp +2.5–3.5%; sales $13.1–13.2B (maintained); EPS $13.55–13.90. RAISED.
- 2024-11-26: comp +3.6–4.2%; sales $13.2–13.3B; EPS $13.65–13.95. RAISED.

- 2025-05-28 (Q1 FY25): maintained; EPS "includes the expected impact from all tariffs currently in effect"; excludes acquisition costs, investment losses, FL results; ~81M (from ~82M). Merger 5/15/25: $24.00 cash or 0.1168 DKS sh; equity value ~$2.4B, EV ~$2.5B; close 2H 2025; financing cash, revolver, new debt to extent of cash elections.
- 2025-08-28 (Q2 FY25): comp +2.0–3.5%; sales $13.75–13.95B; EPS $13.90–14.50; ~81M; ~25% (from ~24%). RAISED. FL holders approved; all regulatory approvals; close anticipated Sep 8, 2025.
- 2025-11-25 (Q3 FY25) FY2025 DICK'S Business only: comp +3.5–4.0%; sales $13.95–14.0B; EPS $14.25–14.55 (excl FL results, investment gains, M&I; ~81M sh excl 9.6M acquisition sh); consolidated Q4 ~91M; tax ~24% DICK'S Bus, consolidated Q4 ~29%; capex ~$1.2B/~$1.0B DICK'S Bus. RAISED (comp from +2.0–3.5%; sales from $13.75–13.95B; EPS from $13.90–14.50; tax from ~25%). FL Q4 FY25: GM down 1,000–1,500bps vs FL reported Q4 LY; PF comp down MSD-HSD; Q4 operating profit "slightly negative" ex one-time costs. Future pretax charges $500–750M (unproductive inventory, store closures, right-sizing, M&I). Consideration $2.5B ($2.1B stock / 9.6M sh, $223.0M cash, $111.6M pre-existing stake).
- 2026-03-12 (Q4 FY25) FY2026 initial: DICK'S comp +2.0–4.0%, FL PF +1.0–3.0%; sales consol $22.1–22.4B, DICK'S $14.5–14.7B, FL $7.6–7.7B; consol OI GAAP $1.71–1.83B, NG $1.68–1.81B; seg profit DICK'S $1.58–1.66B (11.0–11.2%), FL $100–150M (1.3–1.9%); GAAP EPS $13.70–14.70; NG $13.50–14.50; ~91M (incl 9.6M FL sh); ~25.5%; capex ~$1.7B / ~$1.5B. Charges $500–750M total; $235.5M in Q4 FY25, $390.0M FY25. Recon: GAAP OI $1,710–1,833M + $150M FL acquisition costs − $175M litigation & other settlements (interchange + landlord lease termination) = NG $1,685–1,808M; EPS +$1.24 / −$1.43. Dividend +3% to $1.25/qtr ($5.00). Opening plan ~14 HoS, ~22 FH.
- 2026-05-27 (Q1 FY26): DICK'S +2.5–4.0%; FL PF +1.5–3.0%; sales unch; consol OI GAAP $1.69–1.81B, NG $1.71–1.83B; DICK'S $1.60–1.68B (11.0–11.4%); FL $110–150M (1.4–1.9%); GAAP EPS $13.27–14.27; NG $13.50–14.50; ~90.5M; ~27% (unfavorable foreign mix; Europe losses w/ valuation allowances); capex ~$1.6B / ~$1.4B (DICK'S ~$1.2B/~$1.0B; FL ~$0.4B/~$0.4B). Charges $96.5M Q1; $486.5M to date; ~$200M expected FY26 of $500–750M; Fast Break ~100 stores, target ~250 by BTS. Recon: GAAP OI $1,688–1,806M + $200M FL costs − $174M settlements = $1,714–1,832M; EPS +$1.68 / −$1.45. MIXED: comp low ends raised; DICK'S seg profit raised; FL low end raised; NG OI raised; NG EPS maintained; GAAP OI & GAAP EPS cut; tax up; capex down.
- 2026-08-25 (Q2 FY26): DICK'S +2.5–4.0%; FL PF (2.0)% to 0.0%; sales consol $21.9–22.2B, DICK'S $14.5–14.7B, FL $7.4–7.5B; consol OI GAAP $1.45–1.55B, NG $1.46–1.56B; DICK'S $1.54–1.60B (10.6–10.9%); FL $(80)M to $(40)M ((1.1)% to (0.5)%); GAAP EPS $10.94–11.94; NG $11.00–12.00; ~90M; ~29%; capex ~$1.6B / ~$1.4B (DICK'S ~$1.2B/~$1.0B; FL ~$0.4B/~$0.4B). Charges $125.8M in 26 wks; $515.8M to date; total "up to $750 million," ~$200M FY26. IEEPA refunds $59.0M received (+$2.1M interest); $38.1M attributable to prior yr excluded from NG. Recon: GAAP OI $1,448–1,553M + $200M FL costs − $174M settlements − $38M tariff refunds + $21M store operating model redesign = $1,457–1,562M; EPS +$1.68 / −$1.46 / −$0.33 / +$0.17. ### 5. Foot Locker segment detail
- 5.1 Transaction/closing: merger agmt 5/15/25, $24.00 cash or 0.1168 DKS sh (no min/max); equity value ~$2.4B; EV ~$2.5B. Prelim election (Aug 29, 2025 deadline): ~92.6% elected stock (incl 31.6% via guaranteed delivery); ~1.2% cash; ~6.2% no election (incl ~4.5% owned by DKS, cancelled for no consideration) (2025-09-02). FL shareholder approval Aug 22, 2025; HSR expired 11:59pm ET Aug 25, 2025. Closing Sept 8, 2025; combined >3,200 stores in 20 countries; Ed Stack leads FL; Ann Freeman (ex-Nike) Pres FL NA; Matthew Barnes (ex-Aldi Süd/Tesco UK) Pres FL Intl effective Dec 3, 2025. Total consideration $2.5B = $2.1B share (9.6M DKS sh) + $223.0M cash + $111.6M pre-existing equity (4.3M FL sh per PF). Cash acquired net of cash paid $257,095K. Synergies $100M–$125M cost synergies "in the medium term, primarily through procurement and direct sourcing efficiencies"; accretive to EPS in FY2026 ex transaction & one-time costs (2025-09-08). Bridge: $2.4B sr unsecured 364-day bridge term loan commitment (GS Bank, May 2025; joinders May 30, 2025); reduced to $1.75B June 2025 after consents [note: SRC 01 proxy says $2.15B after revolver]; bridge fees paid $7,863K; $7.9M deferred financing amortization expensed FY25. FL 4.000% Sr Notes due 2029 ($400M): exchange + consent solicitation commenced June 6, 2025 ($970 + $30 consent per $1,000); $369,381K (92.35%) tendered at early participation date (June 23, 2025); settled Sept 11, 2025: $381.9M DKS 4.000% Sr Notes due Oct 1, 2029 issued; $18.1M remained FL notes; carrying $383.5M (Q3 FY25) → $384.5M → $385.5M → $386.4M (Q2 FY26). Goodwill $000 245,857 → 699,352 (Q3 FY25) → 864,047 (Q4) → 813,069 (Q1 FY26) → 837,019 (Q2); intangibles 58,598 → 796,715 → 768,575 → 765,371 → 763,252 (measurement-period changes).
- Pro forma (no synergies): FY2024 combined net sales $21,430,849K; NI $1,083,476K (FL FY24 sales $7,988,000K; NI $18,000K). 26 wks to Aug 2, 2025 combined net sales $10,472,293K; NI $256,315K (FL 26-wk sales $3,651,000K; NI $(401,000)K incl $110M goodwill impairment and $291M "impairment and other"). FY2025 combined (2026-09-21): net sales $21,781,120K; GP $6,950,801K; OI $975,958K; NI $563,950K; dil EPS $6.21 on 90,808K sh (FL 26-wk sales $3,647,000K / OI $(297,000)K; FL Aug 3–Sep 8, 2025 stub sales $919,000K / OI $(10,000)K; $62.3M one-time transaction costs noted in Sept-2025 PF).

### 7. Caveats & cross-reference
- Markdown extraction garbled in places (numbers split, "%" dropped); figures from numeric cells, % checked vs "% of Sales." 2025-11-25 header "Weighted average diluted shares outstanding ... 87 ... 83 ... 4 ... 5%" (rounded M); exact 87,115 / 82,776 from IS.
- Q3/Q4 FY22 store counts not in those releases (store tables start 2023-05-23); from beginning/PY columns of later releases; Q4 FY22 specialty breakdown from 2024-03-14 footnote.
- Store-count basis changed twice: from FY2025 Warehouse Sale locations (29 at Feb 1, 2025; 1.3M sq ft) incl in counts and in GGG (FY24 YE 856 → restated beginning FY25 885); one FY24 FH opening previously counted as DICK'S reclassified (FY24 YE 678 DICK'S / 26 FH vs FY25 beginning 677 / 27). Q3 FY24 release only total DSG (727) w/ HoS 17, FH 22.
- Comp definitions changed (relocated FY22; GameChanger FY24; Warehouse Sale FY25; DICK'S Business basis from Q3 FY25 w/ FL excluded until Q4 FY26). FY2024 calendar shift: comps like calendar weeks, reported sales not (Q1 +~$45M, Q2 +~$95M, Q3 −~$105M, Q4 −~$30M; neutral for yr).
- SG&A/pre-opening reclass from FY2024 ($20,578K FY23); NG SG&A for Q4 FY22, FY22, Q3 FY23, Q4 FY23, FY23 restated for deferred-comp reclass (later figure used).
- NG EBIT before FY2024 derived (company didn't present NG op income until 2025-03-11 release): NG GP − NG SG&A (latest) − pre-opening (reclassified).
- Q2 FY26 FL derived net capex ($75,241K) > derived gross ($74,367K) because FL construction allowances fell from $1,175K (Q1 YTD) to $301K (26-wk YTD) → negative Q2 allowance $(874)K; as printed.
- Q3 FY25 investment-gain tax effect (15,702) on 6,376 pretax due to $10.8M favorable item — as printed.
- FL seg GM/profit margins derived; consolidated NG GM excludes FL inventory write-downs (Q4 FY25 $217.9M; Q1 FY26 $42.7M; Q2 FY26 $(2.3)M credit) reported in corporate, so FL segment GP already excludes them.
- FY2023 53rd wk: Q4 FY23 14 wks ($170,223K; ~$0.19 EPS); Q4 FY24 +0.5% vs 14-wk base.
- Guidance recon rounding: FY26 per-share adjustments don't always add (2026-03-12 "Column does not add due to rounding"); litigation settlement $(175)M in 2026-03-12, $(174)M in later (recorded $174,464K).
- PF figures (2025-09-05, 2026-09-21) use FL historicals reclassified + transaction accounting; exclude synergies; "for illustrative purposes only"; not comparable to reported FL segment.
- Cross-check vs transcript file: call figures match derived PR values — Q3 FY24 net capex ~$185M (185,575), dividends $90M, buyback $6.7M @ $194.22 (release YTD avg $203.98; qtr-only price from call); Q1 FY25 net capex $242M; Q2 FY25 $213M, dividends $96M; Q3 FY25 $218M, $109M; Q4 FY25 $302M, $108M, buyback 218,000 sh / $43M @ $199.51; Q1 FY26 $289M, $114M, 719,000 sh / $141M @ $196.38; Q2 FY26 $325M, $111M. NG P&L matches: Q3 FY25 consol NG EBIT $242.2M / 5.81%, DICK'S $288.6M / 8.92%, FL loss $46.3M; Q4 FY25 DICK'S $444.5M / 10.97%; FY25 DICK'S EPS $14.58; Q1 FY26 DICK'S $361M / 10.69%, FL $17.5M / 0.98%; Q2 FY26 DICK'S $485.2M / 12.6%, FL loss $31.9M.
- Only on calls/slides (not in this file): merch-margin bps; ticket/transaction splits (except releases state Q1 FY23 "2.7% increase in transactions", Q2 FY23 "2.8%", FY23 "1.6%"); EBT/op-margin gdnc midpoints (~11.1–11.2%) FY24–FY25; pre-opening $ gdnc ($65–75M; ~$90M); interest gdnc; revolver size ($1.6B → $2B); GameChanger metrics; "pro forma full-Q3 FL OI ~$6.8M"; FL NA comp (−2.6% Q3 FY25; +1.4% Q1 FY26); Fast Break counts after Q1 FY26.
- Only in PRs: exact bridges, store counts by banner, restated PY comps, FY22–FY23 history (transcripts begin Q3 FY24), Nov-2023 EBT margin gdnc, PF statements, notes-exchange & election mechanics. Initial FY25 gdnc confirmed verbatim: comp +1.0–3.0%; sales $13.6–13.9B; EPS $13.80–14.40; ~82M; ~24%; capex ~$1.2B / ~$1.0B.

- FY ends Saturday closest to Jan 31; fiscal 2025 ended Jan 31, 2026; FY2023 53 wks to Feb 3, 2024. FL closed Sep 8, 2025.
- Caveat: 8-K markdowns contain only 8-K body (cover, Items, signature, exhibit index); no exhibit text (Ex. 99.1 PRs, Ex. 99.2 PF tables, Ex. 10.1 agreements) → earnings numbers, PF lines, prelim Q1 FY25 "not in filing."

### 10-Ks
- FY2022 10-K (filed 2023-03-23; 52 wks to Jan 28, 2023; also FY21, FY20): post-pandemic normalization; net sales $12,368,198K, NI $1,043,138K, dil EPS $10.78. 3.25% Convertible Sr Notes due 2025 largely exchanged Dec 2022 ($515.9M in FY22); $1.5B 3.15%/4.10% sr notes (Jan 2022); $2.0B Dec 2021 buyback authorization, $1.428B remaining; dividends $1.95/sh (FY21 had $5.50 special); Nike ~23% of purchases; risk factors still carry convertible/warrant risks.
- FY2023 10-K (2024-03-28; 53 wks to Feb 3, 2024): record yr w/ 53rd wk; sales $12,984,399K, NI $1,046,519K, dil EPS $12.18. Remaining $59.1M converts settled in shares (1.7M sh), hedge/warrants unwound; $649.8M buybacks (5,439K sh); $4.00 dividends; Business Optimization $84.8M pretax ($62.8M after tax, $0.73/dil sh; $72.8M in SG&A; incl $46.1M impairments w/ $4.6M goodwill & $2.2M trademark, $26.7M severance, $12.0M inventory); Nike ~24%; new inventory-shrink risk factor; securities class action filed Feb 16, 2024.
- FY2024 10-K (2025-03-27; 52 wks to Feb 1, 2025): sales $13,442,849K, NI $1,165,308K, dil EPS $14.05; comps +5.2% (revised basis). HoS 19 / FH 26 / Performance Centers 24 at FYE; FY25 capex gdnc "approximately $1 billion, net of construction allowances"; new $3B buyback (Mar 10, 2025) subsequent event; $511.5M remaining on 2021 program; dividends $4.40; Nike ~25%; tariff language added to risk factors; CISO change (Oct 2024 departure, Jan 2025 hire); new "failure to meet market expectations" stock-price risk; first yr ASU 2023-07 segment expense disclosure. Filed same day as FL 10-K; 2 mo before merger announcement.
- FY2025 10-K (2026-03-27; 52 wks to Jan 31, 2026; FL from Sep 8, 2025): first 2-segment 10-K. Sales $17,215,120K (DICK'S $14,108,943K; FL $3,106,177K); NI $849,239K; dil EPS $9.97; DICK'S comps +4.5%. FL PPA (consideration $2,512,662K; goodwill $618,840K; trade names $710,000K; preliminary); FL segment loss $(52,220)K; $390.0M acquisition charges ($217.9M inventory write-down, $164.2M M&I, $7.9M bridge) vs $500–750M expectation; synergies $100–125M. Debt: $1.9B sr notes (4.00% 2029 $400M, 3.15% 2032 $750M, 4.10% 2052 $750M); $2.0B revolver (Jun 2030); $500M CP program. Leases $5.84B liability. Capex $1,137.2M gross; FY26 gdnc ~$1.5B net; FY26 outlook sales $22.1–22.4B, EPS $13.70–14.70. Nike ~31% of consolidated purchases. Risk factors extensively rewritten (FL integration, international, malls, tariffs, AI-enabled cyber threats). Subsequent events: FL HQ/position eliminations ($40–55M), $150M interchange settlement gain, $25M lease termination gain, $1.25 dividend.

- Q2 FY26 (2026-09-03; to Aug 1, 2026): IEEPA refunds $59.0M (COGS reduction; $38.1M prior-yr) + $2.1M interest; measurement-period adj ($19.0M net goodwill decrease in 26 wks); $15.3M DICK'S store operating model redesign; $29.3M Q2 FL acquisition costs ($125.8M 26-wk incl $85.4M M&I and $40.4M inventory charges); supply-chain finance liabilities $25.8M; latest 10-Q before Sept 2026 PF 8-K & $1B notes.

- 2022-11-21/22: Q3 FY22 release; dividend $0.4875.
- 2022-12-07/08 (1.01, 1.02, 3.02): 3.25% Converts due 2025 ($575M original): Exchange Agreements for $95,307,000 principal (~61.7% of outstanding) for cash + shares (VWAP averaging from Dec 8, 2022; §4(a)(2)); ~$59M to remain; annual interest −~$3.1M; partial/complete early terminations of note hedge & warrants (unnamed counterparties); HudsonWest LLC advisor; amounts not in filing.
- 2023-02-09 (7.01): called all $59,127,000 remaining converts; redemption Apr 18, 2023 at 100% + accrued; conversions settled in shares + cash for fractions; unwind hedge/warrants; offset dilution with hedge shares & buybacks.
- 2023-03-06/07: Q4 FY22 release; dividend $0.4875 → $1.00 (+105%).
- 2023-03-02 (eff Feb 10, 2023)/03-08 (1.02): Warrant Early Termination Agreement w/ Wells Fargo Bank N.A.; VWAP-determined shares netted vs counterparty call-option delivery; no penalties.
- 2023-03-21/23 (5.02(e)): 2023 LT performance units (grant Apr 3, 2023; vest Apr 3, 2025 on FY2023-24 total sales, adj EBT, adj merch margin; 0–200%). Targets: Edward W. Stack $1,500,000; Lauren R. Hobart $2,500,000; Navdeep Gupta $1,250,000; Vlad Rak $1,250,000.
- 2023-04-18/24: redemption done: $58,990,000 converted (shares), $137,000 cash; remaining call options/warrants (Wells Fargo) unwound Apr 20, 2023; interest −~$1.9M/yr (FY23 10-K: 1.7M sh, $58.5M to APIC).
- 2023-06-14/16 (5.03, 5.07): 2023 AGM: 11 directors elected; say-on-pay 273,595,183 / 2,309,041; annual frequency; Deloitte; charter amendment for officer exculpation 256,556,984 / 19,280,856 (charter, not bylaw).
- 2023-08-21/22 (2.05): Q2 FY23 release; positions eliminated primarily at customer support center Aug 21, 2023; ~$20M severance Q3 FY23 (cash over 12 mo); further $25–50M business-optimization charges possible, excluded from NG outlook.
- 2023-09-13/15: Robert W. Eddy appointed to Board (Governance & Nominating, Compensation); $180,000 equity award vesting at 1 yr + prorated cash retainer.
- 2024-03-13/14 (7.01): Q4 FY23 release; comp change from FY2024: GameChanger rev included; renamed "comparable store sales" → "comparable sales"; "Comparable store sales measure the growth in sales for the same store location for a particular period from the corresponding period in the prior year and includes both brick and mortar and eCommerce sales." Recast FY23 comps Ex. 99.2. Dividend → $1.10.
- 2024-06-12/13 (5.07): 2024 AGM: 12 directors (Eddy added); say-on-pay 271,771,556 / 2,701,238; Deloitte; EEO-1 disclosure proposal 32,103,219 for / 242,198,917 against; business-judgment-rule bylaw proposal 74,531 for; not approved.
- 2024-08-21/28 (8.01, expressly not 1.05): "On August 21, 2024, the Company discovered unauthorized third-party access to its information systems, including portions of its systems containing certain confidential information." Response plan, external experts, federal law enforcement notified; no known disruption; "the Company believes that this incident is not material"; reassess under 1.05 if facts change.
- 2025-03-10/11 (7.01): Q4 FY24 release; from FY2025 Warehouse Sale stores (previously temporary, excluded) incl in comps from 14th full month and in store counts/sq ft; new definition: "Comparable sales measure the growth in sales compared to the corresponding prior year period for its recurring revenue sources, including sales at the same store location as well as its digital revenue, including eCommerce sales, omni-channel transactions which are fulfilled by its stores, GameChanger subscriptions and its DICK'S media network." Dividend → $1.2125; new 5-yr up to $3B buyback authorized Mar 10, 2025 in addition to Dec 16, 2021 $2B program.
- 2025-03-25/27 (5.02(e)): 2025 LT performance units (grant Apr 3, 2025; vest Apr 3, 2028 on FY2025-26 total sales, adj EBT, external merch margin %, eCommerce sales growth; 0–200%). Targets: Stack $5,000,000; Hobart $3,500,000; Gupta $1,500,000; Raymond A. Sliva $1,250,000; Vlad Rak $1,250,000.
- 2025-05-15 (2.02, 8.01; Rule 425): prelim Q1 FY25 + merger announcement (RJS Sub LLC Merger Sub); exhibits not included.
- 2025-05-15 (1.01): merger terms: $24.00 cash or 0.1168 DKS sh (no cap/proration); employee RSUs/PSUs → DKS time-based RSUs; director RSUs cashed; ITM options cashed at $24 less strike; 2/3 FL vote, HSR & other antitrust, S-4; GS Bank USA commitment up to $2.4B sr bridge; Outside Date May 15, 2026 w/ two 3-mo auto ext; fees $59,500,000 (FL→DKS), $95,500,000 (DKS→FL on antitrust failure).
- 2025-06-06 (1.01, 1.02, 2.03): new $2.0B unsecured revolving Credit Agreement (Wells Fargo Bank agent), $75M LC sublimit, matures June 6, 2030, ABR + 0.125% or adj SOFR + 1.125% initially (ratings-based), max lease-adjusted leverage covenant; terminated Jan 14, 2022 $1.6B facility. Launched exchange of FL 4.000% notes 2029 into up to $400M new DKS 4.000% 2029 + consent payment; FY24 PF furnished (not included).
- 2025-06-11/13 (5.03, 5.07): 2025 AGM: 12 directors; say-on-pay 268,190,052 / 3,051,456; Deloitte; charter amendment increasing authorized common & Class B 238,061,813 / 39,719,997 (FY25 10-K: 1,000,000,000 common, 200,000,000 Class B authorized); affirmative-action risk report proposal not approved.
- 2025-06-23: exchange early participation (amounts n/a); $30.00 early premium per $1,000 extended to all tenders (total $1,000 per $1,000); PF updated for Q1 FY25.
- 2025-07-23/24: DKS voluntarily withdrew & refiled (~Jul 25, 2025) HSR to give FTC more time (new 30-day period); still 2H 2025. S-4 (No. 333-288244) filed Jun 23, amended Jul 8, effective Jul 10, 2025; prospectus & FL DEFM14A filed/mailed Jul 11, 2025.
- 2025-08-26: HSR expired 11:59pm ET Aug 25, 2025; close expected Sep 8; election deadline 5:00pm ET Aug 29, 2025 (Aug 27 for FL 401(k)/Puerto Rico plan participants).
- 2025-08-27/28: Q2 FY25 release. 2025-09-02: prelim election PR (% not in filing). 2025-09-05: exchange recap (FL supplemental indenture Jun 20, 2025 stripping substantially all restrictive covenants); PF updated for Q2 FY25.
- 2025-09-08 (2.01, 7.01): consummated. Final elections ~85.8% stock, ~1.2% cash, ~12.9% no election (incl ~4.5% DKS-owned cancelled; other non-electors cash). Aggregate consideration (ex awards): 9,579,640 whole DKS sh + $222,961,814.59 cash (cash on hand). FL financials & PF by amendment within 71 days.
- 2025-09-11 (1.01, 2.03): exchange settled: $381,932,000 FL 4.000% 2029 tendered/accepted (95.48% computed); $18,068,000 remain at FL; DKS issued $381,932,000 4.000% Sr Notes due Oct 1, 2029 (Jan 14, 2022 Base Indenture / Second Supplemental; U.S. Bank Trust Co. trustee); registration rights w/ Goldman Sachs & Co. (registered exchange by Sep 12, 2026 or additional interest).
- 2025-11-24/25: Q3 FY25 release; Matthew Barnes President FL International eff Dec 3, 2025.
- 2026-03-11/12: Q4 FY25; dividend → $1.25. 2026-05-26/27: Q1 FY26.
- 2026-06-10/12 (5.07): 2026 AGM: 11 directors (Larry D. Stone retired; Board to 11); say-on-pay 280,925,384 / 2,544,487; Deloitte FY26; women's-rights business-risk report proposal 166,344 for, not approved.
- 2026-08-24/25: Q2 FY26 release.
- 2026-09-21 (8.01, 9.01(b)): unaudited PF combined for FYE Jan 31, 2026 (exhibit not included). FY25 10-K Note 2 PF FY2025: net sales $21,786,502K, NI $755,469K, dil EPS $8.30. Filed day before notes offering (presumably for S-3ASR).
- 2026-09-22/25 (1.01): Underwriting Agreement (BofA Securities, PNC Capital Markets, Wells Fargo Securities) $1,000,000,000: $400,000,000 6.200% due Sep 25, 2036 + $600,000,000 6.900% due Sep 25, 2056; unsecured, unsubordinated, no sub guarantees; Jan 14, 2022 Base Indenture + Third Supplemental Sep 25, 2026; S-3ASR No. 333-299029; use: "general corporate purposes, which may include financing operations, repayment of debt, repurchases of shares of common stock and future business acquisitions." Price/yield/net proceeds not in filing. PF sr notes $2.9B ($400M 2029, $750M 2032, $400M 2036, $750M 2052, $600M 2056; computed).

### FL deal timeline additions (beyond above)
- 2025-08-22 FL holders adopted merger agmt (FY25 10-K Note 2).
- 2025-09-08 accounting (FY25 10-K Note 2): total consideration $2,512,662K = stock $2,144,211K + cash $222,962K + previously held 4.3M FL sh $111,632K + replacement awards $29,032K + award settlements $4,825K; goodwill $618,840K; trade names $710,000K; debt assumed $420,760K; FL contributed net sales $3.1B and net loss $60.0M in FY2025.

- 2026-03-23/27 (10-K Item 9B/Note 19): FL position eliminations & relocation to New York HQ ~$40–55M charges Q1 FY26 within $500–750M ($390.0M in FY25); ~$150M expected in FY26; synergies $100–125M medium term; Q4 FY25 goodwill adjustment +$160.4M; FY26 FL outlook PF comps +1% to +3%, seg profit $100–150M; Fast Break ~250 stores by BTS 2026.
- 2026-06-04 / 09-03 10-Qs: $54.3M of interchange settlement to FL purchase price; 26-wk goodwill −$19.0M measurement-period; $44.9M HQ-restructuring charges in 26 wks; litigation contingencies main open PPA area (measurement period ends Sep 2026).

## SRC 00_DKS_SYNTHESIS/DKS_Street_Ratings_PT_and_Estimate_Revision_History.md | undated synthesis (notes 2025-08-30 to 2026-10-02) | consolidation of 45 broker/3rd-party notes in `03_DKS_STREET_RESEARCH\`: JPM (Christopher Horvers), Barclays (Adrienne Yih), Wells Fargo (Ike Boruchow), UBS (Michael Lasser), Williams Trading (Sam Poser), Paragon Intel, InsiderInsights/InsiderTrade, Bloomberg Intelligence credit | ratings/PT/estimates Aug-2025–Oct-2026
- Dates = date printed inside note (filename/archive date often few days later). Garbled tables flagged not guessed.
- FY conventions: DKS fiscal 2025 = FYE ~Jan 31, 2026; 2026 = ~Jan 2027; 2027 = ~Jan 2028. JPM labels by DKS FY in text/model ("Summary of Financials" page shifts one yr: "FY26E" there = fiscal 2025); Barclays FY25–FY28 = DKS FY, PT on "CY27"/"FY27" = fiscal 2027; Wells 2025E–2028E/"FY27" = DKS FY; UBS by FYE month ("01/26E" = fiscal 2025, "01/27E" = FY26, "01/28E" = FY27, "01/29E" = FY28; text "FY'26" = fiscal 2026; PT "~16x FY'27E" = 01/28E). EPS adjusted unless stated; "consolidated" = DKS+FL post 9/8/25; "core DKS"/"DSG" = legacy.

### B.5 Williams Trading — Poser
- 13-Mar-26 (archived 19-Mar): PT $180 → $195; estimates raised (not in OCR). OCR one-page teaser: "Despite better than expected 4Q25 results from both Dick's and Foot Locker, it's too soon [to] get constructive." Core "remains strong, and healthy, but it's too early to tell if Foot Locker will work over the long term." FY26 rev guide above cons but EPS guide high end $0.30 below cons; back-half weighted, driven by new product assortments; most spring '26 FL orders written pre-acquisition. 4Q25 core SSS +3.1% (+9.5% 2-yr), core EPS +11.9% to $4.05; FL SSS better than expected on aggressive promotions to clear stale inventory; FL −$0.16 on 4Q consolidated EPS; deal share count −$0.44.
- 28-May-26: PT $195 → $215; raised (not extracted); 1Q26 adj EPS "$0.90 vs consensus $0.91" (verified vs PDF: broker's own typo). 1Q26 consolidated SSS +4.1% (Dick's +6.0%, FL +0.6%); FY26 EPS/rev guide maintained; FL NA +1.4% led by +6.4% at Foot Locker stores, WSS weak, Kids FL/Champs lesser, intl down on Europe; 100 Fast Break stores drove DD comps; 250 by BTS, more by holiday; BTS buys made by new organization. Core "strong and healthy"; "too early to tell if Foot Locker will work over the long term"; expects FL SSS improvement from BTS but unsure if sustainable "or just a bounce off the bottom".

### E. Wells Fargo "Sporting Goods Retail Primer: The X's and O's of the Industry and Its Playmakers" (Aug 10, 2026; file 2026-08-17; 68 pp; Boruchow, Paul Kearney, Juliana Duque, Robert Bischoff). DKS sections pp.5–28 duplicate upgrade note; industry pp.42–62. Many exhibits extracted as loose numbers.
- E.1: DKS OW $240 (14.5x FY27 $16.50; up/down $285/$150). ASO EW $50 (7.5x FY27 GAAP EPS $6.54; FY26 $6.11; up/down $70/$30). NKE EW $40 (22.5x CY27 EPS $1.75). $150–250B US mkt, +LSD, consolidated, shifted to softlines/brands/omni, Amazon ~13% for most of a decade; demand "as healthy as it has been in two decades."
- E.2 Demand: daily sports/exercise 23% of US pop (all-time high; <20% two decades ago); annual participation ~75%; inactivity <20% first time after 7 consecutive yrs of decline (SFIA: 250M participated ≥once 2025, ~+1% y/y; team sports 90M+; pickleball fastest-growing 5 yrs). Sports/exercise hours 6.6% of leisure 2025 vs 5.7% 2003–07; per-participant share fell 31% → 27% (more casual). Age 15–19 13.3% (+172bps vs LT avg); 45–54 7.0% (+162bps). Youth NSCH (6–17) 58% 2024 from 49% 2021; 42% below poverty vs 75% at 400%+ of poverty; avg primary-sport spend $1,016 2024 (+46% vs 2019, ~2x inflation), ~$1,500 incl secondary; $100k+ HH $2,361/child vs $890 <$49k; family youth-sports spend >$40B/yr (Aspen); state range ~49–74%; growth strongest in lower-cost recreational formats (Southwest). Outdoor (OIA 2025): record 181M in 2024 (>60% of pop 6+, from 50% 2007; +3% y/y); seniors +7.4%, youth +5.6%, Black +12.8%, Hispanic +11.8%; $100k+ HH +5% 4-yr CAGR; 2020–24 West South Central +840bps, Middle Atlantic +680bps, Mountain +670bps; avg participant age 34.7 → 38.8; hikers do other activities 85% of time. Wellness: US wellness economy $2.1T, +8% CAGR 2019–24 (GWI), $6.3K/person; record 77M gym members (23% of pop >6, +20% vs 2019), ~96M incl non-members; Google Trends health/wellness/nutrition/gym at/near highs; GLP-1, "MAHA."
- E.3: TAM: DKS core US ~$140B, global $300B; ASO US $245B ($125B sporting goods + $45B outdoor/western lifestyle + $40B work/outdoor rec + $35B hunting/fishing). DKS ~9.0% US share 2025 (6.5% global); ASO ~5.0%. Euromonitor B&M +2% CAGR 2016–25 (+2.4% total growth CAGR). Share 2016 → 2025 =same as SRC 02 §2.1 table, plus split Bass Pro 7.4% → 7.8%, Cabela's 5.8% → 3.9%. Growth CAGR 2016–25: DKS 5.4%, FL −2.9%, Bass Pro 3.0%, Cabela's −2.0%, ASO 1.7%, Pentland 10.1%, REI −0.2%. 2025 y/y: DKS +6.3%, FL −3.1%, ASO −0.1%, total +0.8%. Positioning: DKS athlete/premium/experiential; REI outdoor/challenger; Cabela's hunting; Bass fishing; JD athletic softlines; Academy everyday family value vs mass.
- E.4 Consolidation (27 events 2003–2026): Gart/Sports Authority 2003 (~385 stores, ~$2.5B); DKS-Galyan's 2004 (~$305M, 47 stores); Leonard Green LBO Sports Authority 2006 (~$1.3B); DKS-Golf Galaxy 2007 ($18.82/sh, ~$225M); DKS-Chick's 2007 (~$71M, 15 stores); G.I. Joe's liquidation 2009; KKR-Academy 2011 (~$2.2B); Sports Authority liquidation 2016 (~460 stores; DKS bought 31 leases + IP ~$15M); Vestis/Sport Chalet/EMS 2016; Golfsmith 2016 (36 stores → Golf Galaxy → 98 stores); Performance Sports Group 2016 (~$575M); Bass Pro-Cabela's 2016–17 (~$4.0B equity at $61.50, ~$5B EV, ~1.1x sales/~10x EBITDA); Gander Mountain-Camping World 2017 (~$390M); JD-Finish Line 2018 ($13.50/sh, ~$558M, ~0.3x sales); Performance Bicycle 2018; Modell's 2020 (~134 stores); FL-WSS 2021 (~$750M, ~100 stores); FL-atmos 2021 (~$360M); Bass Pro-Sportsman's Warehouse terminated 2021 ($18/sh, ~$785M, FTC); Fleet Feet-Marathon Sports 2022; JD-Hibbett 2024 ($87.50/sh, ~$1.1B, ~0.6x sales/~6x EBITDA, ~1,170 doors); Mountain Sports (EMS/Bob's) liquidation 2024; DKS-FL 2025 ($2.5B EV; $24.00 or 0.1168; ~86% premium to $12.87; closed 9/8/25; ~0.3x sales, ~6–7x depressed LTM EBITDA; "the defining event of the cycle"); Big 5 take-private 2025 (~$1.45/sh, ~$112M); Moosejaw/Next Adventure closures 2025; Eddie Bauer Ch.11 2026 (180 stores).
- E.5: DKS softlines 43% (FY06) → 63% (FY24: hardlines 37% / apparel 34% / footwear 29%) → 76% PF FY25 w/ FL (hardlines 24% / apparel 27% / footwear 49%). ASO 47% softlines since FY17 (FY25 sports & rec 22%, outdoor 30%, apparel 27%, footwear 20%). JD 90%+ softlines. Nike ~25% of core DKS (12% FY06; ~20% FY15–16); FL ~55–60% Nike 2025; combined ~36–37%; ASO Nike 12% (14% FY17). Private label: core DKS ~13% (10% historically; 15% peak FY20); consolidated PF ~11% (FL historically ~50% of apparel); ASO ~22% (goal >25%; >50% of customers bought PL). Brand access = primary reason ASO under-comps (DKS Nike +36% FY22 drove footwear +16% while ASO Nike −6%). E-com: DKS consolidated ~16% (DSG 12% FY17, 29% FY20, ~15% FY24); 70% of sales omni customers, 90% store-enabled, 80% online orders store-fulfilled; profit-neutral after 2017 in-housing. ASO 12% (4% FY17; target 15% in 5 yrs); ASO 1Q26 e-com +17% vs B&M +1%. Amazon (E-Marketer, ex generic athletic apparel/footwear): category $70.9B (2016) → $132.8B (2025) → $141.4B (2026E); e-com penetration 23.8% → 39.8% → 40.1%; Amazon category sales $6.6B (2018) → $18.4B (2026E); Amazon share 8.9% (2018), 12.2% (2020), 13.0% (2026E), ~32% of category e-com. Footprint: DKS 888 at end-2025 (every state except MT, AK, HI); ASO 322 (117 in Texas, >1/3). Since 2019 DKS opened most in TX (8), FL (5); ASO moving north (8 IN, 5 NC, 3 OH, 3 VA, 2 PA). State-weighted: DKS states youth participation 58.6% vs ASO 54.1% (2024; US 58.0%); median income per capita $73K vs $66K.
- E.6 Macro: sporting equipment PCE +5.1%/yr 20-yr avg (+4% ex-COVID); deflationary 83% of months since 2000; 2026 YTD price +3.5%, units +6.0%, total +9.6%; June 2026 +12.0% (price +3.0%, units +8.9%); sporting goods ~2.0% of goods PCE vs ~1.6% hist (2.2% COVID peak). Elasticity (2000–present ex-2020/21): sporting goods/guns/ammo −1.35 (= children's clothing) vs all goods −0.23, footwear −0.68, clothing −0.51, recreational items −0.85. Census sporting goods retail +3%/yr avg since 2010; 2022–25 −1% to −4%/yr (lagging retail ex-autos & GAFO); 2026 YTD +7% vs retail +5%, GAFO +4%; accel began early 2025 thru May 2026.
- E.7 ASO: comps FY22 −6.4%, FY23 −6.5%, FY24 −5.1%, FY25 −1.5%; FY26E +1.1%; 1Q26 +2.9% entirely ticket (+4.5%) vs transactions −1.5%; sales/sq ft $252 FY25 vs $276 FY17; SG&A/sq ft $75 vs $73; lease-adj ROIC 10.1% FY25, down every yr from 22.0% FY21; GM 34–35% since FY21 (FY25 34.8%). LRP 450 stores (~25/yr, +$1.9B sales, ~$15M/store), 800+ LT; "outside-in"; 55K sq ft boxes (from 63K); Yr-1 sales ~$16M legacy / ~$14M existing / ~$12M new markets, maturing ~$20M; 2022–24 vintages comping +MSD in 2025 (~70bps comp lift → ~100bps); WF waterfall implies legacy comps still negative (−1.2% to −2.0% FY26–28). ~$300M from retail media/loyalty/personalization/brands/in-stocks; retail media ~30bps margin; 70% of ASO customers don't shop other leading sporting-goods retailers; AI imagery +30% views/+10% conversion/+20% orders; app MAU +40% in 2 yrs. Lowest quintile spends 73% on essentials; 10% gasoline increase = 140bps discretionary headwind bottom quintile vs 80bps overall.
- E.8 DKS: core DSG GM ~700bps above 2019 (~360bps merch, ~310bps occupancy, ~30bps other); OM ~600bps higher (FY19 5.1% → FY25 11.1%). GM FY15–FY27E: 30.0/30.4/29.1/28.9/29.3/31.8/38.3/34.6/35.0/35.9/36.3/36.6/36.7%; OM FY15–FY27E: 7.5/6.9/5.9/5.3/5.1/7.7/16.5/12.1/10.6/11.1/11.1/11.2/11.2%. Sales/sq ft $317 (from $208 pre-COVID; +~50%); occupancy/sq ft % of sales −300bps+; SG&A/sq ft $84 (from $45–49 FY15–19).
- DSG comp disaggregation (relo/conversion + GC + core): FY24 5.2% = 2.2% + 0.2% + 2.8%; FY25 4.5% = 2.3% + 0.4% + 1.9%; FY26E 3.7% = 2.2% + 0.3% + 1.2%; FY27E 4.1% = 2.7% + 0.3% + 1.1%; FY28E 3.6% = 2.1% + 0.3% + 1.2%. FY25 sq ft +440bps (320bps true new, 120bps relocations: −360bps DSG, +180bps FH, +300bps HoS). Comp revisions: relocations (FY22, +90bps FY21 restated), GC (FY24, +20bps FY23), warehouse (FY25).
- HoS/FH (WF est): HoS $35.0M sales, 47.5% MM, occupancy $3.6M ($30/sq ft), labor $5.5M (168 employees), 4-wall EBITDA $7.5M (21%), investment $27.5M, ROIC 27.2%, payback 3.7 yrs, $292/sq ft (124% of DSG $235); FH $14.0M, EBITDA $3.2M (23%), investment $7.5M, ROIC 42.1%, payback 2.4 yrs, $280/sq ft (119%). Sensitivity: HoS ROIC 7.7% ($25M sales, 45% MM) to 56.7% ($45M, 55% MM); FH 13.4% to 85.4%.
- Other: GGG 52 stores (21 + 29 converted warehouses); GC $150M FY25, +40% CAGR, ~10M users, 4M+ MAU, est +18bps DSG OM by FY27 at 30% OM; ScoreCard =D.5; DMN "other" revenue $383M bucket, <1% of sales.
- FL in primer: Nike penetration 56% (FY07) → 75% (FY20) → 59% (FY24) → ~57% (FY25E); next-4 brands 16% (FY20) → 28% (FY25E); FL comped positive 11 of 19 yrs since 2007, only 3 while Nike declined; Nike high-heat ~40% → ~20% (launch/atomic 7–8%); FY21 → FY23: Nike high-heat sales $1,613M → $735M, FL sales $8,961M → $8,168M, markdown rate 16.0% → 17.7%, total margin impact ~491bps; FL store base 2,227 (1Q26), Champs 364, WSS 100; ~850 A/B and 450+ C/D mall stores (FY22, plan ~630/<250); >75% of Champs within a mile of FL; FL intl ~30% of 2024 sales.
